  Symptoms: ['swollen, hot, painful joint', 'refusal to move the joint', 'fever', 'limp', 'irritability']
- appendicitis_child (Acute Appendicitis in Children) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['abdominal pain starting around the umbilicus, migrating to the right lower quadrant', 'nausea, vomiting', 'loss of appetite', 'fever', "tenderness at McBurney's point", 'rebound tenderness', 'guarding']
- thalassemia_child (Thalassemia Major (Beta-Thalassemia)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['severe anemia presenting in early infancy (3-6 months)', 'pale skin, poor feeding, growth retardation', 'jaundice', 'hepatosplenomegaly (large liver and spleen)', 'prominent forehead (frontal bossing)', 'maxillary overgrowth', 'severe bone deformities']
- sickle_cell_disease (Sickle Cell Disease in Children) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['sickle cell crises (severe pain in bones, chest, abdomen)', 'fever', 'dactylitis (painful hand-foot swelling in infants)', 'pallor, jaundice', 'splenic sequestration (sudden enlargement of spleen, shock)', 'acute chest syndrome (fever, chest pain, respiratory distress)']
- malrotation_newborn (Malrotation with Volvulus (Neonatal)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['bilious vomiting (green)', 'abdominal distension', 'failure to pass meconium', 'blood in stool', 'lethargy, shock']
- tracheoesophageal_fistula (Tracheoesophageal Fistula (TEF)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['excessive drooling', 'coughing and choking with first feed', 'cyanosis with feeding', 'aspiration', 'respiratory distress', 'difficulty passing a feeding tube']
- diaphragmatic_hernia (Congenital Diaphragmatic Hernia (CDH)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['respiratory distress at birth', 'scaphoid abdomen (concave belly)', 'bowel sounds in chest', 'cyanosis', 'shift of heart sounds to the right']
- pediatric_diabetes_type1 (Type 1 Diabetes in Children) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['polyuria (frequent urination)', 'polydipsia (excessive thirst)', 'polyphagia (extreme hunger)', 'unexplained weight loss', 'fatigue', 'bedwetting (new onset)', 'blurry vision', 'fruity breath (DKA)']
- pediatric_celiac_crisis (Celiac Crisis (Severe Celiac Disease)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['profuse diarrhea', 'severe dehydration', 'electrolyte imbalance (hypokalemia)', 'acidosis', 'malnutrition', 'weight loss', 'abdominal distension', 'lethargy']
- diabetes_mellitus_type2 (Type 2 Diabetes Mellitus) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['frequent urination (polyuria)', 'excessive thirst (polydipsia)', 'increased hunger (polyphagia)', 'unexplained weight loss', 'fatigue and weakness', 'blurred vision', 'slow-healing wounds', 'tingling or numbness in hands and feet', 'recurrent skin or urinary tract infections']
- diabetes_mellitus_type1 (Type 1 Diabetes Mellitus) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['extreme thirst (polydipsia)', 'frequent urination (polyuria)', 'rapid unintentional weight loss', 'extreme hunger', 'blurred vision', 'severe fatigue', 'fruity-smelling breath (acetone)', 'nausea and vomiting (in DKA)']
- coronary_artery_disease (Coronary Artery Disease (CAD) – Chronic Angina) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['chest pain or pressure (angina) that occurs with exertion or stress', 'pain radiating to left arm, jaw, neck, or back', 'shortness of breath', 'palpitations', 'fatigue', 'dizziness', 'indigestion or heartburn-like sensation', 'angina usually subsides with rest or nitroglycerin']
- chronic_heart_failure (Chronic Heart Failure (Congestive Heart Failure)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['shortness of breath (dyspnea) on exertion or at rest', 'orthopnea (shortness of breath when lying flat)', 'paroxysmal nocturnal dyspnea (waking up gasping for air)', 'ankle and leg swelling (edema)', 'fatigue and weakness', 'palpitations', 'persistent cough or wheezing', 'reduced exercise tolerance', 'weight gain (fluid retention)']
- atrial_fibrillation (Atrial Fibrillation (AF)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['palpitations (irregular, fast heartbeat)', 'dizziness, lightheadedness', 'shortness of breath', 'fatigue', 'chest discomfort', 'may be asymptomatic and detected incidentally', 'risk of stroke (clots in the heart go to the brain)']
- rheumatic_heart_disease (Rheumatic Heart Disease (RHD)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['history of acute rheumatic fever in childhood', 'mitral stenosis (breathlessness, palpitations, hemoptysis)', 'mitral regurgitation (fatigue, palpitations)', 'aortic valve involvement (chest pain, syncope)', 'heart failure symptoms (edema, dyspnea)', 'atrial fibrillation (palpitations)']
- dilated_cardiomyopathy (Dilated Cardiomyopathy (DCM)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['progressive breathlessness (dyspnea)', 'fatigue, weakness', 'ankle edema', 'palpitations', 'chest pain', 'orthopnea', 'reduced exercise capacity', 'may be idiopathic, post-viral, alcoholic, or familial']
- hypertrophic_cardiomyopathy (Hypertrophic Cardiomyopathy (HCM)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['chest pain (angina)', 'shortness of breath (especially on exertion)', 'palpitations', 'dizziness or syncope (fainting)', 'sudden cardiac death (risk in young athletes)', 'may be asymptomatic', 'family history is common']
- peripheral_arterial_disease (Peripheral Arterial Disease (PAD)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['leg pain or cramping when walking (intermittent claudication)', 'relieved by rest', 'cold feet, pale or blue skin', 'non-healing ulcers or sores on feet/legs', 'absent or weak pulses in legs', 'hair loss on legs and feet', 'brittle toenails', 'erectile dysfunction (men)']
- carotid_artery_stenosis (Carotid Artery Stenosis) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['often asymptomatic', 'transient ischemic attacks (TIA): temporary weakness, numbness, or difficulty speaking', 'amaurosis fugax (temporary vision loss in one eye)', 'increased risk of stroke', 'detected on carotid Doppler']
- chronic_kidney_disease (Chronic Kidney Disease (CKD) Stage 3-5) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['fatigue, weakness', 'swelling (edema) of ankles, feet, and face', 'decreased urine output', 'foamy or dark urine', 'high blood pressure', 'nausea, vomiting', 'loss of appetite, metallic taste', 'itching (pruritus)', 'muscle cramps', 'difficulty concentrating', 'shortness of breath (fluid overload)']
- chronic_glomerulonephritis (Chronic Glomerulonephritis (GN)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['hematuria (blood in urine)', 'proteinuria (foamy urine)', 'hypertension', 'edema (swelling)', 'reduced kidney function (elevated creatinine)', 'fatigue, anemia', 'may be asymptomatic until advanced']
- copd (Chronic Obstructive Pulmonary Disease (COPD)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['chronic cough with mucus (sputum)', 'shortness of breath, especially on exertion', 'wheezing', 'chest tightness', 'frequent respiratory infections', 'fatigue', 'cyanosis (bluish lips) in advanced stages', 'barrel-shaped chest', 'history of smoking or biomass fuel exposure']
- idiopathic_pulmonary_fibrosis (Idiopathic Pulmonary Fibrosis (IPF)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['progressive shortness of breath (dyspnea)', 'dry, persistent cough', 'fatigue', 'weight loss', 'clubbing of fingers', 'basilar crackles on auscultation', 'older age (usually >50 years)']
- pulmonary_arterial_hypertension (Pulmonary Arterial Hypertension (PAH)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['shortness of breath (dyspnea on exertion)', 'fatigue', 'dizziness or syncope (fainting) on exertion', 'chest pain', 'palpitations', 'ankle edema', 'cyanosis (blue lips/fingers)']
- chronic_liver_disease (Chronic Liver Disease / Cirrhosis) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['fatigue, weakness', 'jaundice (yellowing of skin and eyes)', 'abdominal distension (ascites)', 'ankle swelling (edema)', 'easy bruising and bleeding', 'spider angiomas (spider-like blood vessels on skin)', 'palmar erythema (red palms)', 'confusion or forgetfulness (hepatic encephalopathy)', 'vomiting blood (esophageal varices)', 'loss of appetite, weight loss']
- alcoholic_liver_disease (Alcoholic Liver Disease (ALD)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['fatigue, jaundice', 'ascites (abdominal swelling)', 'edema (ankle swelling)', 'nausea, vomiting', 'loss of appetite, weight loss', 'hepatomegaly (enlarged liver)', 'in advanced: cirrhosis, encephalopathy, variceal bleeding']
- primary_sclerosing_cholangitis (Primary Sclerosing Cholangitis (PSC)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['fatigue', 'pruritus (itching)', 'jaundice', 'abdominal pain (right upper quadrant)', 'recurrent cholangitis (fever, chills)', 'often associated with inflammatory bowel disease (IBD)']
- chronic_pancreatitis (Chronic Pancreatitis) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['chronic abdominal pain (epigastric, radiating to back)', 'pain worsens after meals', 'weight loss', 'steatorrhea (fatty, foul-smelling stools)', 'diabetes (pancreatic endocrine insufficiency)', 'nausea, vomiting']
- crohns_disease (Crohn's Disease (Inflammatory Bowel Disease)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['chronic diarrhea (may be bloody)', 'abdominal pain (often in right lower quadrant)', 'weight loss', 'fever', 'fatigue', 'perianal disease (fistulas, abscesses)', 'extraintestinal manifestations (joint pain, skin lesions, eye inflammation)']
- ulcerative_colitis (Ulcerative Colitis (UC)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['bloody diarrhea with mucus', 'abdominal pain and cramping', 'rectal urgency and tenesmus', 'weight loss, fatigue', 'fever', 'extraintestinal manifestations (arthritis, skin, eye)']
- peptic_ulcer_disease (Peptic Ulcer Disease (Chronic)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['burning epigastric pain (worse on empty stomach, relieved by food or antacids)', 'heartburn', 'bloating', 'nausea', 'weight loss', 'hematemesis (vomiting blood) or melena (black stools) if bleeding']
- rheumatoid_arthritis_adult (Rheumatoid Arthritis (Adult)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['symmetrical joint pain and swelling (same joints on both sides)', 'morning stiffness >1 hour', 'warm, red, swollen joints', 'fatigue, fever, weight loss', 'joint deformity (swan neck, boutonnière) if untreated', 'rheumatoid nodules', 'affects small joints of hands and feet']
- systemic_lupus_erythematosus (Systemic Lupus Erythematosus (SLE)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['malar rash (butterfly-shaped rash on face)', 'discoid rash (scaly patches)', 'photosensitivity', 'oral/nasal ulcers', 'arthritis (non-erosive)', 'serositis (pleuritis, pericarditis)', 'renal involvement (nephritis, proteinuria)', 'neurological (seizures, psychosis)', 'hematologic (anemia, thrombocytopenia)', 'positive ANA and other autoantibodies']
- systemic_sclerosis (Systemic Sclerosis (Scleroderma)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['skin thickening (scleroderma)', "Raynaud's phenomenon (fingers turn white/blue in cold)", 'digestive issues (GERD, dysphagia, diarrhea/constipation)', 'pulmonary fibrosis or pulmonary hypertension', 'renal crisis (hypertension, renal failure)', 'calcinosis, telangiectasia', 'anti-centromere or anti-Scl-70 antibodies']
- polymyalgia_rheumatica (Polymyalgia Rheumatica (PMR)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['severe muscle pain and stiffness in shoulders, hips, and neck', 'morning stiffness >1 hour', 'difficulty getting up from a chair, raising arms', 'fever, weight loss, malaise', 'elevated ESR and CRP', 'usually in patients >50 years']
- hyperthyroidism_graves (Graves' Disease (Hyperthyroidism)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['weight loss despite increased appetite', 'palpitations, rapid heart rate', 'heat intolerance, excessive sweating', 'tremor (fine hand tremor)', 'nervousness, anxiety', 'goiter (enlarged thyroid)', "proptosis (bulging eyes—Graves' ophthalmopathy)", 'thickening of skin on shins (pretibial myxedema)']
- multiple_sclerosis (Multiple Sclerosis (MS)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['optic neuritis (painful vision loss in one eye)', 'numbness, tingling in limbs', 'muscle weakness (often one side)', 'difficulty walking, ataxia', 'bladder and bowel dysfunction', 'fatigue (very common)', 'cognitive impairment', "Lhermitte's sign (electric shock sensation on neck flexion)"]
- amyotrophic_lateral_sclerosis (Amyotrophic Lateral Sclerosis (ALS / Motor Neuron Disease)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['progressive muscle weakness (starts in limbs or bulbar)', 'fasciculations (muscle twitching)', 'cramps', 'spasticity', 'dysarthria (slurred speech)', 'dysphagia (difficulty swallowing)', 'respiratory insufficiency', 'no cognitive impairment in most cases', 'upper and lower motor neuron signs']
- myasthenia_gravis (Myasthenia Gravis (MG)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['fluctuating muscle weakness (worse with exertion, improves with rest)', 'ptosis (drooping eyelid)', 'diplopia (double vision)', 'difficulty chewing, swallowing', 'slurred speech (dysarthria)', 'difficulty breathing (myasthenic crisis)', 'weakness of proximal limbs']
- chronic_epilepsy_adult (Chronic Epilepsy (Adult)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['recurrent unprovoked seizures', 'types: generalized tonic-clonic (jerking, loss of consciousness), focal (partial, with or without impaired awareness), absence (brief staring spells)', 'post-ictal confusion, headache', 'may have aura before seizure', 'risk of injuries during seizures']
- cluster_headache (Cluster Headache (Chronic)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['severe, strictly unilateral pain (orbital, supraorbital, or temporal)', 'lasting 15-180 minutes', 'frequency: once every other day to 8 times per day', 'associated with ipsilateral autonomic symptoms (red/watery eye, nasal congestion, ptosis, miosis)', 'restlessness or agitation during attacks', 'circannual and circadian periodicity (cluster periods)']
- trigeminal_neuralgia (Trigeminal Neuralgia (Chronic)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['sudden, severe, sharp, stabbing, electric shock-like pain in the distribution of one or more branches of the trigeminal nerve (face, jaw, eye)', 'triggered by trivial stimuli (talking, chewing, brushing teeth, cold air)', 'pain lasts seconds to 2 minutes', 'remission periods may occur', 'often unilateral']
- sickle_cell_anemia_adult (Sickle Cell Anemia (Adult)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['chronic hemolytic anemia (fatigue, pallor, jaundice)', 'sickle cell crises (severe pain in bones, chest, abdomen)', 'hand-foot syndrome (dactylitis in children, less in adults)', 'acute chest syndrome (chest pain, fever, respiratory distress)', 'splenic sequestration (sudden splenomegaly, shock)', 'stroke risk (especially in children)', 'chronic complications: avascular necrosis, leg ulcers, retinopathy']
- thalassemia_major_adult (Thalassemia Major (Adult)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe transfusion-dependent anemia', 'pallor, fatigue, weakness', 'jaundice', 'hepatosplenomegaly', 'iron overload (due to repeated transfusions): cardiac, hepatic, endocrine dysfunction', 'bone deformities (frontal bossing, maxillary overgrowth)', 'growth retardation']
- hemophilia_a (Hemophilia A (Factor VIII Deficiency)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['spontaneous bleeding into joints (hemarthrosis) causing pain, swelling, and deformity', 'muscle bleeds', 'prolonged bleeding after trauma or surgery', 'easy bruising', 'hematuria (blood in urine)', 'intracranial hemorrhage (head trauma is dangerous)']
- hemophilia_b (Hemophilia B (Factor IX Deficiency)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['same as Hemophilia A (joint bleeds, muscle bleeds, easy bruising)', 'prolonged bleeding', 'less common than Hemophilia A']
- diabetic_peripheral_neuropathy (Diabetic Peripheral Neuropathy) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['numbness, tingling, burning pain in hands and feet (stocking-glove distribution)', 'sharp, shooting pain', 'loss of sensation (danger: cannot feel wounds)', 'weakness of foot muscles', 'foot ulcers and infections', 'decreased reflexes']
- diabetic_retinopathy (Diabetic Retinopathy (Non-Proliferative and Proliferative)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['floaters (spots in vision)', 'blurred vision', 'fluctuating vision', 'loss of visual field', 'sudden vision loss (vitreous hemorrhage, retinal detachment)', 'often asymptomatic until advanced']
- diabetic_nephropathy (Diabetic Nephropathy) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['early: microalbuminuria (detected on urine test, no symptoms)', 'later: proteinuria (foamy urine), edema, hypertension', 'decreased eGFR, elevated creatinine', 'fatigue, anemia', 'end-stage renal disease (dialysis needed)']
- diabetic_foot_ulcer (Diabetic Foot Ulcer (Chronic)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['non-healing sore or ulcer on the foot', 'surrounding redness, warmth, or swelling', 'discharge (pus) or odor', 'pain (may be absent due to neuropathy)', 'gangrene (black, dead tissue)', 'fever (if infection spreads)']
- primary_open_angle_glaucoma (Primary Open-Angle Glaucoma (POAG)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['often asymptomatic in early stages (silent thief of sight)', 'gradual loss of peripheral vision (tunnel vision)', 'optic disc cupping on fundoscopy', 'elevated intraocular pressure (IOP)', 'family history is a risk factor']
- marfan_syndrome (Marfan Syndrome) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['tall stature, long limbs (arm span > height)', 'arachnodactyly (long, slender fingers)', 'pectus excavatum or carinatum', 'joint hypermobility', 'lens dislocation (ectopia lentis)', 'aortic dilation/aneurysm', 'mitral valve prolapse', 'dural ectasia (lower back pain)']
- polycythemia_vera (Polycythemia Vera (PV)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['headache, dizziness, and fatigue', 'itching (pruritus), especially after a hot shower (aquagenic pruritus)', 'plethora (ruddy complexion)', 'splenomegaly', 'hypertension', 'hyperviscosity symptoms (visual disturbances, tinnitus)', 'high risk of thrombosis (stroke, DVT, MI)']
- myelofibrosis (Primary Myelofibrosis) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['fatigue, weakness (anemia)', 'fever, night sweats, weight loss', 'splenomegaly (massive—abdominal pain, early satiety)', 'bone pain', 'pruritus', 'leukopenia or thrombocytopenia', 'tear-drop poikilocytes on blood film', 'karyotype abnormalities']
- chronic_myeloid_leukemia (Chronic Myeloid Leukemia (CML)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['fatigue, weakness, weight loss', 'night sweats', 'fever', 'splenomegaly (abdominal fullness)', 'elevated white blood cell count', 'easily bleeding or bruising', 'usually in chronic phase at diagnosis']
- myelodysplastic_syndrome (Myelodysplastic Syndrome (MDS)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['fatigue, pallor (anemia)', 'recurrent infections (neutropenia)', 'easy bruising, bleeding (thrombocytopenia)', 'may be asymptomatic', 'progressive bone marrow failure']
- multiple_myeloma (Multiple Myeloma) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['bone pain (often back, ribs, skull—lytic lesions)', 'fatigue, weakness, pallor (anemia)', 'recurrent infections (immunoparesis)', 'renal failure (elevated creatinine, proteinuria)', 'hypercalcemia (nausea, confusion, polyuria, polydipsia)', 'weight loss', 'fractures (pathological)']
- polyneuropathy_chronic (Chronic Inflammatory Demyelinating Polyneuropathy (CIDP)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['progressive, symmetric muscle weakness (proximal and distal)', 'numbness, tingling in hands and feet', 'loss of reflexes (areflexia)', 'difficulty walking', 'sensory ataxia (poor coordination)', 'progression over >8 weeks (differentiates from Guillain-Barré)']
- autoimmune_hemolytic_anemia (Autoimmune Hemolytic Anemia (Chronic)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['fatigue, weakness, pallor (anemia)', 'jaundice (yellow skin and eyes)', 'dark urine', 'splenomegaly', 'fever', 'positive Coombs test', 'chronic or acute exacerbations']
- oral_submucous_fibrosis (Oral Submucous Fibrosis (Chronic)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['progressive difficulty opening mouth (trismus)', 'burning sensation in the mouth, especially with spicy food', 'blanching, pale mucosa with fibrous bands', 'soft palate involvement', 'dysphagia (difficulty swallowing)', 'precancerous condition']
- leukoplakia (Oral Leukoplakia (Chronic Precancerous Lesion)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['white patches or plaques in the mouth that cannot be scraped off', 'usually asymptomatic', 'most common on buccal mucosa, tongue, floor of mouth', 'associated with tobacco/smoking', 'risk of malignant transformation (1-10%)']
- oral_cancer (Oral Cancer (Squamous Cell Carcinoma)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['non-healing ulcer or growth in the mouth', 'white or red patch', 'persistent pain, bleeding', 'loosening of teeth', 'difficulty chewing, swallowing, or speaking', 'unilateral ear pain', 'neck lymphadenopathy', 'weight loss']
- asthma (Asthma) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['wheezing', 'shortness of breath', 'chest tightness', 'cough especially at night', 'symptoms worsen with exercise or cold air', 'difficulty sleeping due to breathlessness']
- measles (Measles) -> Urgency: high

- elder_002 (Osteoporotic Fracture (Hip/Vertebral)) -> Urgency: critical

- elder_005 (Delirium (Acute Confusional State)) -> Urgency: critical

- elder_011 (Geriatric Depression) -> Urgency: high

  Symptoms: ['progressive weakness, anorexia', 'increasing sleep, withdrawal', 'cool extremities, mottling', 'changes in breathing (Cheyne‑Stokes)']
- em_001 (Cardiac Arrest – Out‑of‑Hospital) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden collapse', 'unresponsiveness', 'no normal breathing', 'no pulse', 'gasping respirations (agonal breathing)', 'cyanosis (blue skin)']
- em_002 (Acute Myocardial Infarction (Heart Attack)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['crushing chest pain or pressure, lasting >20 minutes', 'pain radiating to left arm, jaw, neck, or back', 'shortness of breath', 'cold sweats, nausea, vomiting', 'dizziness or lightheadedness', 'feeling of impending doom', 'atypical symptoms in women: fatigue, jaw pain, epigastric pain']
- em_003 (Acute Heart Failure / Pulmonary Oedema) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe shortness of breath (dyspnoea) at rest or on minimal exertion', 'orthopnoea (cannot lie flat)', 'paroxysmal nocturnal dyspnoea (waking up gasping)', 'pink, frothy sputum', 'profuse sweating', 'anxiety, restlessness', 'crackles on lung auscultation', 'rapid heart rate']
- em_004 (Hypertensive Emergency (Malignant Hypertension)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['very high blood pressure (>180/120 mmHg)', 'severe headache', 'blurred vision or visual loss', 'chest pain', 'shortness of breath', 'nausea, vomiting', 'seizures', 'altered consciousness']
- em_005 (Aortic Dissection (Acute)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden, severe, tearing chest or back pain', 'pain that migrates or radiates', 'syncope (fainting)', 'pulse deficit (difference in BP between arms)', 'neurological deficits (stroke, paraplegia)', 'shortness of breath', 'hoarseness', 'diaphoresis']
- em_006 (Pulmonary Embolism (Massive)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden severe shortness of breath', 'sharp chest pain (pleuritic)', 'tachycardia (rapid heart rate)', 'cough with haemoptysis (coughing blood)', 'dizziness, syncope', 'low oxygen saturation', 'signs of DVT (leg swelling, pain) in some cases', 'hypotension (if massive)']
- em_007 (Cardiac Tamponade (Acute)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['muffled heart sounds', 'distended neck veins', 'hypotension (low BP)', 'paradoxical pulse (BP drops during inspiration)', 'chest pain, shortness of breath', 'anxiety, restlessness', 'signs of shock']
- em_008 (Supraventricular Tachycardia (SVT) – Unstable) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden onset of rapid, regular heart rate (150–250 bpm)', 'palpitations', 'dizziness, lightheadedness', 'chest pain', 'shortness of breath', 'hypotension (if unstable)', 'syncope or near‑syncope']
- em_009 (Ventricular Tachycardia / Fibrillation (Cardiac Arrest)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden collapse', 'unconsciousness', 'no pulse', 'no breathing', 'seizure‑like activity (in some)', 'agonal gasps']
- em_010 (Acute Severe Bradycardia (Heart Block)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['slow heart rate (<40 bpm)', 'dizziness, syncope', 'chest pain', 'shortness of breath', 'hypotension', 'confusion', 'fatigue']
- em_011 (Status Epilepticus (Prolonged Seizure)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['seizure lasting more than 5 minutes', 'seizures occurring without recovery of consciousness', 'generalized tonic‑clonic movements', 'focal seizures with impaired awareness', 'cyanosis', 'injuries from convulsion']
- em_012 (Acute Stroke (Ischemic or Haemorrhagic)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden numbness or weakness of face, arm, or leg (one side)', 'sudden confusion, trouble speaking or understanding', 'sudden trouble seeing in one or both eyes', 'sudden severe headache with no known cause', 'sudden trouble walking, dizziness, loss of balance', 'FAST: Face, Arms, Speech, Time']
- em_013 (Subarachnoid Haemorrhage (Brain Bleed)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ["sudden, severe, 'thunderclap' headache", 'neck stiffness', 'photophobia (sensitivity to light)', 'nausea, vomiting', 'loss of consciousness or confusion', 'seizures', 'focal neurological deficits']
- em_014 (Traumatic Brain Injury (Severe)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['loss of consciousness (any duration)', 'persistent headache', 'vomiting', 'unequal pupils', 'seizures', 'clear fluid from nose or ears (CSF leak)', 'confusion, agitation, or drowsiness', 'numbness or weakness']
- em_015 (Spinal Cord Injury (Acute)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['neck or back pain after trauma', 'numbness, tingling, or weakness in limbs', 'inability to move arms or legs (paralysis)', 'loss of bladder/bowel control', 'priapism (in men)', 'respiratory difficulty (if high cervical injury)']
- em_016 (Acute Meningitis / Encephalitis) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['high fever', 'severe headache', 'neck stiffness (nuchal rigidity)', 'photophobia', 'nausea, vomiting', 'confusion, drowsiness', 'seizures', 'rash (non‑blanching in meningococcal)', 'irritability (in children)']
- em_017 (Severe Asthma (Status Asthmaticus)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe breathlessness, unable to speak in full sentences', 'use of accessory muscles (neck, chest)', 'silent chest (no wheezing – ominous sign)', 'cyanosis (blue lips/fingertips)', 'confusion, exhaustion', 'pulsus paradoxus', 'rescue inhaler not providing relief']
- em_018 (Tension Pneumothorax (Acute)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden severe shortness of breath', 'sharp chest pain (usually unilateral)', 'tachycardia', 'hypotension', 'decreased breath sounds on one side', 'distended neck veins', 'tracheal deviation (late sign)', 'cyanosis']
- em_019 (Flail Chest (Traumatic)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['paradoxical chest wall movement (segment moves inward during inspiration)', 'severe chest pain', 'shortness of breath', 'tachycardia', 'cyanosis', 'history of blunt chest trauma']
- em_020 (Massive Haemoptysis (Coughing Blood)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['coughing up large amounts of bright red blood (>100 ml)', 'shortness of breath', 'dizziness, lightheadedness', 'tachycardia', 'hypotension', 'clots in sputum', 'signs of underlying lung disease (TB, bronchiectasis, cancer)']
- em_021 (Acute Respiratory Distress Syndrome (ARDS)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe shortness of breath (dyspnoea)', 'rapid, shallow breathing', 'low oxygen saturation (hypoxaemia)', 'cyanosis', 'confusion, restlessness', 'crackles on lung examination', 'often precipitated by sepsis, trauma, or pneumonia']
- em_022 (Acute Epiglottitis (Airway Obstruction)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden onset of high fever', 'severe sore throat, difficulty swallowing (dysphagia)', 'drooling (inability to swallow saliva)', 'stridor (high‑pitched respiratory sound)', 'tripod position (leaning forward, mouth open)', 'respiratory distress', 'rapid onset (hours)']
- em_023 (Croup (Acute Laryngotracheobronchitis) – Severe) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['barking cough', 'stridor (inspiratory, worse at night)', 'hoarseness', 'fever', 'respiratory distress (chest retractions, tachypnoea)', 'cyanosis in severe cases', 'usually in children 6 months – 3 years']
- em_024 (Foreign Body Aspiration (Airway Obstruction)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden onset of choking', 'coughing, wheezing, or inability to speak', 'stridor', 'cyanosis', 'respiratory distress', 'the child may have a history of eating small objects or nuts']
- em_025 (Diabetic Ketoacidosis (DKA) – Severe) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['nausea, vomiting, severe abdominal pain', 'fruity‑smelling breath (acetone)', 'deep, rapid breathing (Kussmaul breathing)', 'extreme thirst, frequent urination', 'confusion, drowsiness, or coma', 'high blood sugar (usually >250 mg/dL)', 'positive urine ketones']
- em_026 (Hyperglycaemic Hyperosmolar State (HHS)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['extreme thirst, polyuria', 'weakness, confusion, drowsiness', 'very high blood sugar (>600 mg/dL)', 'severe dehydration (dry mouth, sunken eyes)', 'no or mild ketosis', 'neurological symptoms (seizures, coma)', 'often in Type 2 diabetes with precipitating illness']
- em_027 (Severe Hypoglycaemia (Unconscious)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['unconsciousness or inability to swallow', 'cold, clammy skin', 'sweating', 'tremors', 'confusion before losing consciousness', 'seizures', 'known diabetes and on insulin/oral hypoglycaemics']
- em_028 (Thyroid Storm (Acute Hyperthyroidism)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['high fever (>38.5°C)', 'tachycardia (heart rate >140 bpm)', 'agitation, confusion, or psychosis', 'nausea, vomiting, diarrhoea', 'jaundice', 'profuse sweating', 'arrhythmias (atrial fibrillation)', 'precipitated by infection, trauma, or stopping antithyroid drugs']
- em_029 (Myxedema Coma (Severe Hypothyroidism)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['profound lethargy, confusion, or coma', 'hypothermia (low body temperature)', 'bradycardia (slow heart rate)', 'hypotension', 'hyponatraemia', 'periorbital oedema', 'coarse, dry skin', 'non‑pitting oedema', 'precipitated by cold exposure, infection, or stopping thyroxine']
- em_030 (Adrenal Crisis (Addisonian Crisis)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe fatigue, weakness', 'nausea, vomiting, abdominal pain', 'hypotension, shock', 'fever', 'confusion, coma', "hyperpigmentation of skin (in chronic Addison's)", 'precipitated by infection, surgery, or stress in those on steroids']
- em_031 (Hypercalcaemic Crisis (Severe Hypercalcaemia)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['nausea, vomiting, constipation', 'polyuria, polydipsia (excessive thirst and urination)', 'confusion, lethargy, coma', 'muscle weakness', 'arrhythmias', 'bone pain', 'history of malignancy, hyperparathyroidism, or excessive Vitamin D']
- em_032 (Acute Hyponatraemia (Severe Low Sodium)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['nausea, vomiting', 'headache, confusion', 'seizures', 'coma', 'muscle cramps, weakness', 'increased risk in marathon runners, psychogenic polydipsia, or SIADH']
- em_033 (Acute Hyperkalaemia (Life‑Threatening High Potassium)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['muscle weakness, paralysis', 'palpitations, arrhythmias', 'nausea, vomiting', 'ECG changes (peaked T waves, widened QRS)', 'often asymptomatic until severe', 'risk factors: renal failure, ACE inhibitors, potassium supplements']
- em_034 (Acute Hypokalaemia (Severe Low Potassium)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe muscle weakness, paralysis', 'arrhythmias, palpitations', 'constipation', 'cramps', 'respiratory muscle weakness', 'ECG: flattened T waves, U waves']
- em_035 (Acute Pancreatitis (Severe)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden severe epigastric pain (radiating to back)', 'nausea, vomiting', 'fever', 'tachycardia', 'abdominal tenderness, guarding', 'signs of shock (hypotension, confusion)']
- em_036 (Acute Cholecystitis (Severe)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe right upper quadrant pain (biliary colic) lasting >6 hours', 'fever, chills', 'nausea, vomiting', "positive Murphy's sign (pain on deep inspiration)", 'tachycardia', 'jaundice (if obstruction)']
- em_037 (Acute Appendicitis (Perforation Risk)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ["pain starting in the periumbilical area, migrating to the right lower quadrant (McBurney's point)", 'nausea, vomiting', 'fever', 'rebound tenderness', 'guarding (muscle tightness)', 'loss of appetite']
- em_038 (Bowel Obstruction (Acute)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['colicky abdominal pain (waxing and waning)', 'vomiting (bilious or faeculent)', 'abdominal distension', 'constipation and obstipation (no flatus or stool)', 'tender, tympanic abdomen', 'signs of dehydration']
- em_039 (Gastrointestinal Bleeding – Massive) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['haematemesis (vomiting blood – coffee‑ground or fresh)', 'melena (black, tarry stools)', 'haematochezia (bright red blood per rectum)', 'signs of shock (tachycardia, hypotension, dizziness)', 'pallor, weakness', 'collapse']
- em_040 (Oesophageal Variceal Bleeding (Cirrhosis)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden massive haematemesis (vomiting large amounts of blood)', 'melena', 'signs of shock (hypotension, tachycardia)', 'jaundice (in cirrhosis)', 'ascites', 'altered consciousness (hepatic encephalopathy)']
- em_041 (Acute Renal Failure (Oliguric/Anuric)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden decrease in urine output (<400 ml/day or <0.5 ml/kg/hr)', 'fluid overload (oedema, pulmonary congestion)', 'uraemic symptoms (nausea, confusion, pruritus)', 'hyperkalaemia', 'metabolic acidosis', 'hypertension']
- em_042 (Rhabdomyolysis (Acute Muscle Breakdown)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe muscle pain (especially calves, thighs, lower back)', 'muscle weakness', 'dark red or cola‑coloured urine (myoglobinuria)', 'reduced urine output', 'fever, malaise', 'signs of compartment syndrome', 'caused by crush injury, prolonged immobilisation, statin use, or drugs']
- em_043 (Eclampsia (Seizures in Pregnancy/Preeclampsia)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['generalised tonic‑clonic seizures in a pregnant or postpartum woman', 'high blood pressure', 'proteinuria', 'headache', 'visual disturbances', 'hyperreflexia', 'confusion after seizure']
- em_044 (Postpartum Haemorrhage (Massive Bleeding)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['heavy vaginal bleeding after delivery (soaking 1 pad per hour or more)', 'hypotension (low BP)', 'tachycardia (rapid pulse)', 'dizziness, syncope', 'pale, cold, clammy skin', 'uterus soft and boggy']
- em_045 (Ectopic Pregnancy – Ruptured) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden severe lower abdominal pain (one‑sided, sharp, stabbing)', 'vaginal bleeding (may be light or heavy)', 'shoulder tip pain (referred pain from blood tracking to the diaphragm)', 'syncope (fainting)', 'dizziness, weakness', 'positive pregnancy test', 'signs of internal bleeding (shock)']
- em_046 (Ovarian Torsion (Acute)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden severe one‑sided lower abdominal pain', 'nausea, vomiting', 'pain may be intermittent initially', 'tender adnexal mass on examination', 'fever', 'usually in women with ovarian cysts or PCOS']
- em_047 (Uterine Rupture (in Labour)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden severe abdominal pain (tearing sensation)', 'cessation of uterine contractions', 'vaginal bleeding', 'fetal heart rate abnormalities (bradycardia)', 'signs of shock (hypotension, tachycardia)', 'easy palpation of fetal parts']
- em_048 (Placental Abruption (Acute)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden severe abdominal pain', 'vaginal bleeding (may be concealed)', 'uterine tenderness and rigidity (board‑like abdomen)', 'fetal distress (bradycardia)', 'signs of shock', 'preterm labour or contractions']
- em_049 (Cord Prolapse (Obstetric Emergency)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['umbilical cord visible in the vagina or perineum after membrane rupture', 'fetal heart rate decelerations (variable or prolonged)', 'fetal distress', 'the cord may be pulsating or not']
- em_050 (Amniotic Fluid Embolism (AFE)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden onset of respiratory distress', 'hypotension (low BP)', 'cyanosis (blue colour)', 'cardiac arrest', 'haemorrhage (DIC – bleeding from multiple sites)', 'seizures', 'occurs during labour, delivery, or immediately postpartum']
- em_051 (Severe Burns – Major ( >20% TBSA)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['large areas of skin with deep burns (white, charred, or blistered)', 'severe pain (or no pain in 3rd degree)', 'signs of shock (hypotension, tachycardia)', 'inhalation injury (singed nasal hairs, hoarse voice, sooty sputum)', 'difficulty breathing', 'altered consciousness']
- em_052 (Crush Injury (Traumatic)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe pain, swelling, deformity in the crushed area', 'numbness or tingling', 'pale or blue skin distal to the injury', 'absent pulse distal to the injury (vascular compromise)', 'signs of compartment syndrome (tense, shiny skin, severe pain on passive stretch)']
- em_053 (Amputation – Traumatic (Limb)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['complete severance of a limb or digit', 'severe bleeding from the stump', 'shock (pale, dizzy, tachycardia)', 'pain', 'the amputated part may be recovered or not']
- em_054 (Penetrating Chest Injury (Stab/Gunshot)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['wound in the chest', 'shortness of breath', 'sucking chest wound (air entering through the wound)', 'hypotension', 'tachycardia', 'cyanosis', 'distended neck veins (if tamponade)']
- em_055 (Penetrating Abdominal Injury (Stab/Gunshot)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['wound in the abdomen', 'severe pain', 'signs of internal bleeding (hypotension, tachycardia, pallor)', 'peritoneal signs (rigidity, guarding)', 'evisceration (protrusion of abdominal contents)']
- em_056 (Pelvic Fracture – Unstable) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['history of major trauma', 'pelvic pain', 'inability to bear weight', 'shortening or rotation of a leg', 'signs of internal bleeding (shock)', 'blood at the urethral meatus', 'ecchymosis over the pelvis']
- em_057 (Femur Fracture (Open/Closed)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe thigh pain, deformity (shortened, rotated leg)', 'inability to bear weight', 'swelling, bruising', 'signs of shock (if open or large blood loss)']
- em_058 (Fat Embolism Syndrome (Trauma)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['confusion, agitation', 'respiratory distress (hypoxaemia)', 'petechial rash (especially on chest, axillae, conjunctiva)', 'fever', 'tachycardia', 'occurs 24–72 hours after long bone or pelvic fractures']
- em_059 (Compartment Syndrome (Acute)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe pain out of proportion to the injury', 'pain on passive stretch of the muscles', 'tense, swollen, shiny skin', 'paresthesia (numbness, tingling)', 'decreased pulse (late sign)', 'pallor of the extremity']
- em_060 (Facial Fractures (Le Fort, Orbital)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['facial swelling, bruising, deformity', 'epistaxis (nosebleed)', 'malocclusion (teeth not aligning)', 'diplopia (double vision) if orbital', 'numbness in the face (infraorbital nerve)', 'CSF rhinorrhoea (clear fluid from nose)']
- em_061 (Penetrating Eye Injury (Globe Rupture)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['visible laceration or foreign body in the eye', 'severe pain', 'loss of vision', 'blood in the eye (hyphema)', 'irregular pupil', 'protrusion of intraocular contents']
- em_062 (Acute Angle‑Closure Glaucoma) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden, severe eye pain', 'blurred vision, halos around lights', 'red eye (conjunctival injection)', 'fixed, mid‑dilated pupil', 'nausea, vomiting', 'headache', 'hard (stony) eye on palpation']
- em_063 (Retinal Detachment (Acute)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden onset of flashing lights (photopsia)', 'floaters (black spots, cobwebs) in vision', 'shadow or curtain in the peripheral or central visual field', 'painless', 'progressive vision loss']
- em_064 (Chemical Burn to the Eye (Alkali/Acid)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe pain, redness, tearing', 'blurred vision', 'swelling of eyelids', 'chemical splatter or powder in the eye', 'alkali burns are more severe than acid burns']
- em_065 (Heat Stroke (Exertional) – Emergency) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['core temperature >40°C', 'hot, dry skin (may be sweaty in exertional type)', 'confusion, delirium, seizures, coma', 'tachycardia, hypotension', 'nausea, vomiting', 'central nervous system dysfunction']
- em_066 (Severe Hypothermia (Core Temperature <32°C)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['body temperature <32°C (89.6°F)', 'shivering (may be absent in severe)', 'confusion, slurred speech', 'slow heart rate, slow breathing', 'weak pulse', 'coma', 'skin cold and pale']
- em_067 (Frostbite – Severe (Blisters, Gangrene)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['white, waxy, numb, or hard skin', 'blistering after rewarming', 'blackening of skin (gangrene)', 'loss of sensation', 'joint stiffness']
- em_068 (High‑Altitude Pulmonary Oedema (HAPE)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['breathlessness at rest', 'dry cough progressing to frothy sputum', 'fatigue, weakness', 'tachycardia', 'cyanosis', 'low oxygen saturation', 'occurs at altitudes >2,500 m, often within 2–4 days of ascent']
- em_069 (High‑Altitude Cerebral Oedema (HACE)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe headache', 'ataxia (loss of coordination)', 'confusion, disorientation', 'hallucinations', 'vomiting', 'decreased level of consciousness, coma', 'occurs at altitudes >2,500 m']
- em_070 (Decompression Sickness (Caisson Disease)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['joint pain (bends)', 'dizziness, vertigo', 'numbness, tingling', 'confusion', 'shortness of breath (chokes)', 'skin rash (mottling)', 'paralysis', 'occurs after scuba diving or rapid ascent from depth']
- em_071 (Drowning – Delayed (Secondary Drowning)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['history of submersion', 'persistent coughing, shortness of breath', 'fever', 'confusion, lethargy', 'crackles on lung auscultation', 'symptoms may appear hours after the event']
- em_072 (Lightning Strike – Cardiac/Neurological) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['loss of consciousness', 'cardiac arrest', 'confusion, amnesia', 'seizures', 'burn marks (linear, Lichtenberg figures)', 'ruptured eardrums', 'paralysis', 'signs of trauma from fall']
- em_073 (Electrical Shock – High Voltage) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['loss of consciousness', 'cardiac arrest', 'burns (entry and exit wounds)', 'muscle contractions', 'neurological symptoms (numbness, paralysis)', 'internal organ damage', 'fractures from fall']
- em_074 (Carbon Monoxide Poisoning (Severe)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['headache, dizziness, nausea', 'confusion, loss of consciousness', 'cherry‑red skin (classic but not always present)', 'seizures', 'coma', 'history of being in an enclosed space with a fuel‑burning appliance']
- em_075 (Opioid Overdose (Respiratory Depression)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['pinpoint pupils', 'respiratory depression (slow, shallow breathing, or apnea)', 'cyanosis (blue lips/nails)', 'unconsciousness', 'slow heart rate', 'history of opioid use (heroin, morphine, methadone)']
- em_076 (Acute Benzodiazepine Overdose) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['drowsiness, confusion, ataxia', 'slurred speech', 'hypotension', 'respiratory depression (mild to severe, if combined with alcohol)', 'coma']
- em_077 (Tricyclic Antidepressant (TCA) Overdose) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['agitation, confusion, seizures', 'dilated pupils', 'dry mouth, urinary retention', 'tachycardia, arrhythmias (widened QRS, prolonged QT)', 'hypotension', 'respiratory depression', 'coma']
- em_078 (Paracetamol Overdose (Acute)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['nausea, vomiting (initial phase)', 'then asymptomatic for 12–24 hours', 'then right upper quadrant pain (liver damage)', 'jaundice, confusion (hepatic encephalopathy)', 'elevated liver enzymes, coagulopathy']
- em_079 (Organophosphate Poisoning (Acute)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['DUMBELLS: Diarrhoea, Urination, Miosis (pinpoint pupils), Bradycardia, Bronchospasm, Emesis, Lacrimation, Salivation, Sweating', 'muscle fasciculations, weakness', 'confusion, seizures, coma', 'history of pesticide exposure']
- em_080 (Corrosive Poisoning (Acid/Alkali)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe burning pain in the mouth, throat, and chest', 'drooling, dysphagia (difficulty swallowing)', 'vomiting (bloody)', 'respiratory distress', 'shock', 'perforation (acute abdomen, mediastinitis)']
- em_081 (Snake Bite – Systemic Envenoming (Neurotoxic)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['ptosis (drooping eyelids)', 'diplopia (double vision)', 'dysphagia (difficulty swallowing)', 'dysarthria (slurred speech)', 'descending paralysis', 'respiratory failure', 'history of snake bite (especially krait, cobra)']
- em_082 (Snake Bite – Systemic Envenoming (Haemotoxic)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['bleeding from gums, puncture sites, or wounds', 'haematuria (blood in urine)', 'haematemesis (vomiting blood)', 'ecchymosis (bruising)', 'hypotension, shock', 'history of viper bite', 'local swelling, blistering']
- em_083 (Scorpion Sting – Severe (Cardiovascular)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe pain at the sting site', 'sweating, salivation', 'tachycardia or bradycardia', 'hypertension or hypotension', 'pulmonary oedema', 'priapism (in men)', 'vomiting', 'especially dangerous in children']
- em_084 (Anaphylactic Shock (Severe Allergic Reaction)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden hives, swelling of lips/face/throat', 'difficulty breathing, wheezing', 'dizziness, hypotension', 'tachycardia', 'abdominal pain, vomiting', 'feeling of impending doom', 'loss of consciousness', 'trigger: food, insect sting, medication']
- em_085 (Septic Shock (Severe Sepsis)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['fever or hypothermia', 'tachycardia (rapid heart rate)', 'hypotension (low BP)', 'confusion, altered mental state', 'tachypnoea (rapid breathing)', 'cold, clammy skin or warm, flushed skin', 'decreased urine output', 'signs of infection (pneumonia, UTI, wound)']
- em_086 (Toxic Shock Syndrome (TSS)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden high fever (>39°C)', 'hypotension (shock)', 'sunburn‑like rash (desquamation later)', 'vomiting, diarrhoea', 'muscle aches', 'confusion', 'mucous membrane hyperaemia (red eyes, throat)', 'often associated with tampon use, wounds, or surgery']
- em_087 (Severe Dengue (Dengue Haemorrhagic Fever / DSS)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['high fever, severe headache, retro‑orbital pain', 'abdominal pain, persistent vomiting', 'bleeding (gums, nose, petechiae, haematemesis)', 'lethargy, restlessness', 'hypotension (shock)', 'fluid accumulation (ascites, pleural effusion)']
- em_088 (Severe Malaria (Cerebral / Falciparum)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['high fever, chills, profuse sweating', 'confusion, seizures, coma (cerebral malaria)', 'jaundice (liver dysfunction)', 'haemoglobinuria (blackwater fever – dark urine)', 'anaemia (pallor, fatigue)', 'respiratory distress (acidosis)']
- em_089 (Meningococcaemia (Meningococcal Septicaemia)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['high fever, chills', 'non‑blanching purpuric/petechial rash (classic)', 'headache, neck stiffness', 'hypotension, shock', 'confusion, coma', 'signs of sepsis']
- em_090 (Gas Gangrene (Clostridial Myonecrosis)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe pain at the wound site (out of proportion)', 'crepitus (gas in tissues – crackling sensation)', 'foul‑smelling, thin, dark discharge', 'edema, skin discoloration (bronze to black)', 'systemic toxicity (fever, tachycardia, confusion)']
- em_091 (Testicular Torsion (Acute)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden severe testicular pain (usually unilateral)', 'swelling, tenderness', 'nausea, vomiting', 'abdominal pain', 'high‑riding testis (horizontal lie)', 'loss of cremasteric reflex', 'occurs in adolescents and young adults']
- em_092 (Priapism (Prolonged Erection)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['persistent, painful erection lasting >4 hours', 'not associated with sexual arousal', 'penile pain', 'distal glans is firm, corpora cavernosa rigid', 'flaccid glans', 'can cause ischaemic injury and fibrosis']

  Symptoms: ['inability to urinate despite full bladder', 'severe lower abdominal pain (suprapubic)', 'distended, palpable bladder', 'restlessness, agitation', 'overflow incontinence (dribbling)', 'often in men with BPH']

- em_095 (Suicidal Ideation – Imminent Risk) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['talking about wanting to die or kill oneself', 'making a plan (e.g., acquiring means)', 'saying goodbye to loved ones', 'giving away possessions', 'sudden calmness after severe depression (may have decided)', 'hopelessness, trapped feeling']

- plague (Plague (Yersinia pestis)) -> Urgency: emergency

- anthrax (Anthrax) -> Urgency: high

- leishmaniasis_mucocutaneous (Mucocutaneous Leishmaniasis) -> Urgency: high

- cysticercosis (Neurocysticercosis (Taenia solium)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['seizures (most common)', 'headaches', 'hydrocephalus', 'confusion', 'if cysts die — inflammation, edema, increased intracranial pressure']

- aspergillosis (Aspergillosis) -> Urgency: high

- hantavirus (Hantavirus Pulmonary Syndrome (HPS)) -> Urgency: emergency
  Symptoms: ['fever', 'myalgia', 'headache', 'fatigue', 'then sudden onset dyspnea, cough, hypotension (cardiogenic shock)', 'high mortality']

- ectopic_pregnancy (Ectopic Pregnancy) -> Urgency: critical

- postpartum_depression (Postpartum Depression (PPD)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['persistent sadness or crying after childbirth', 'feeling detached from baby', 'extreme fatigue beyond normal new-parent tiredness', 'anxiety and panic attacks', 'difficulty sleeping even when baby sleeps', 'thoughts of harming self or baby', 'irritability and anger', 'loss of interest in everything']
- breast_abscess (Breast Abscess) -> Urgency: high

- shoulder_dystocia (Shoulder Dystocia (Difficult Delivery of Shoulders)) -> Urgency: critical

- amniotic_fluid_embolism (Amniotic Fluid Embolism (AFE)) -> Urgency: critical

- deep_vein_thrombosis_pregnancy (Deep Vein Thrombosis (DVT) in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: high

- obstetric_cholestasis (Obstetric Cholestasis (Intrahepatic Cholestasis of Pregnancy — ICP)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe itching (pruritus) especially on palms and soles, worse at night', 'no rash', 'jaundice (yellowing of skin/eyes)', 'dark urine, pale stool', 'fatigue, nausea']

- hellp_syndrome (HELLP Syndrome (Hemolysis, Elevated Liver enzymes, Low Platelets)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['right upper quadrant pain', 'nausea, vomiting', 'headache', 'blurred vision', 'malaise', 'bleeding tendency', 'often occurs with preeclampsia']
- pregestational_diabetes (Pregestational Diabetes (Type 1 or Type 2 in Pregnancy)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['polyuria, polydipsia, weight loss (Type 1)', 'fatigue, recurrent infections (Type 2)', 'may be asymptomatic', 'higher risk of congenital anomalies']
- gestational_hypertension (Gestational Hypertension (No Proteinuria)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['BP ≥ 140/90 after 20 weeks without proteinuria', 'usually asymptomatic', 'may progress to preeclampsia']
- postdate_pregnancy (Post-term Pregnancy (≥42 weeks)) -> Urgency: high

- multiple_pregnancy (Multiple Pregnancy (Twins, Triplets)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['excessive nausea/vomiting', 'larger fundal height than dates', 'more fetal movements', 'weight gain more than singleton']
- preterm_premature_rupture (Preterm Premature Rupture of Membranes (PPROM)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['leakage of fluid before 37 weeks', 'vaginal discharge (clear/watery)', 'not associated with contractions', 'may be with or without infection']
- cervical_insufficiency (Cervical Insufficiency (Cervical Incompetence)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['painless cervical dilation in second trimester', 'recurrent second-trimester miscarriages', 'preterm labor', 'often asymptomatic']
- gestational_high_bp (Chronic Hypertension in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['high BP before pregnancy or before 20 weeks', 'may be asymptomatic', 'risk of preeclampsia and fetal growth restriction']
- fetal_growth_restriction (Fetal Growth Restriction (FGR / IUGR)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['fundal height < 2 cm below gestational age', 'decreased fetal movements', 'abnormal Doppler studies (umbilical artery)', 'low birth weight (< 10th percentile)']
- maternal_sepsis (Maternal Sepsis) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['fever (or hypothermia)', 'tachycardia', 'hypotension', 'altered mental state', 'organ dysfunction (renal, hepatic, respiratory)', 'source: chorioamnionitis, endometritis, wound infection']
- obstetric_dic (Disseminated Intravascular Coagulation (DIC) in Obstetrics) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['bleeding from puncture sites, gums, wound, vagina', 'petechiae', 'shock', 'fever', 'laboratory: low platelets, high PT/APTT, low fibrinogen']
- postpartum_eclampsia (Postpartum Eclampsia (Seizures After Delivery)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['seizures occurring within 48 hours to 6 weeks postpartum', 'may have preeclampsia features (or not)', 'headache, visual disturbances before seizure']
- postpartum_cardiomyopathy (Peripartum Cardiomyopathy (PPCM)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['shortness of breath (especially at night)', 'orthopnea', 'edema (legs, feet)', 'palpitations', 'chest pain', 'cough', 'fatigue', 'occurs in last month of pregnancy or up to 5 months postpartum']
- pregnancy_rh_isoimmunization (Rh Isoimmunization (Rh-Negative Mother, Rh-Positive Baby)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['mother is Rh-negative, father Rh-positive', 'fetal anemia, hydrops fetalis', 'jaundice in newborn', 'stillbirth risk']
- pregnancy_hypothyroidism (Hypothyroidism in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['fatigue', 'weight gain', 'cold intolerance', 'constipation', 'dry skin', 'goiter', 'slower heart rate']
- pregnancy_hyperthyroidism (Hyperthyroidism in Pregnancy (Graves' Disease)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['weight loss despite increased appetite', 'palpitations', 'heat intolerance', 'nervousness', 'tremor', 'goiter', 'proptosis (eye bulging)']
- pregnancy_hepatitis_b (Hepatitis B in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['often asymptomatic', 'fatigue, jaundice, nausea', 'may have chronic infection', 'transmission to baby at delivery']
- pregnancy_chickenpox (Chickenpox (Varicella) in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['itchy vesicles, fever, malaise', 'can be severe in pregnancy (pneumonia)', 'risk to fetus: congenital varicella syndrome (limb hypoplasia, skin scarring, eye defects) if infection in early pregnancy']
- pregnancy_influenza (Influenza in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['fever, cough, body aches, fatigue, sore throat', 'pregnancy increases risk of severe disease (pneumonia, ICU admission)']
- pregnancy_zika (Zika Virus in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['fever, rash, joint pain, red eyes', 'often asymptomatic', 'risk of microcephaly and other birth defects']
- pregnancy_parvovirus_b19 (Parvovirus B19 (Fifth Disease) in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['mild rash, fever, joint pain', 'fetal: risk of hydrops fetalis (severe anemia) if infected in first half of pregnancy']
- pregnancy_listeriosis (Listeriosis in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['fever, muscle aches, sometimes GI symptoms', 'can be asymptomatic', 'fetal: abortion, premature delivery, neonatal sepsis']
- pregnancy_tuberculosis (Tuberculosis in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['cough > 2 weeks, fever, night sweats, weight loss', 'may be asymptomatic', 'can cause preterm labor and low birth weight']
- pregnancy_malaria (Malaria in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['fever, chills, headache, myalgia', 'can cause severe anemia, preterm labor, low birth weight, maternal death']
- pregnancy_dengue (Dengue in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['high fever, headache, joint pain, rash', 'risk of severe dengue (plasma leakage, hemorrhage)', 'can cause preterm labor, abruptio placentae']
- pregnancy_zika (Zika Virus in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['fever, rash, joint pain, red eyes', 'often asymptomatic', 'risk of microcephaly and other birth defects']
- pregnancy_typhoid (Typhoid Fever in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['prolonged fever, headache, constipation/diarrhea, abdominal pain, rose spots']
- pregnancy_hepatitis_e (Hepatitis E in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['acute hepatitis: fever, nausea, vomiting, jaundice, dark urine', 'particularly severe in third trimester (fulminant hepatitis, mortality 20-30%)']
- pregnancy_appendicitis (Appendicitis in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['abdominal pain (may be in right lower quadrant, but location varies with gestational age)', 'nausea, vomiting', 'fever', 'rebound tenderness', 'laboratory: leukocytosis (normal in pregnancy, so difficult)']
- pregnancy_pancreatitis (Acute Pancreatitis in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe epigastric pain', 'radiating to back', 'nausea, vomiting', 'fever', 'tachycardia', 'risk factors: gallstones, hyperlipidemia, alcohol']
- pregnancy_itching (Pruritus in Pregnancy (Without Rash)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['generalized itching, worse at night', 'no visible skin rash', 'may be due to obstetric cholestasis (bile acids)', 'or due to dry skin, cholestasis']
- pregnancy_domestic_violence (Domestic Violence in Pregnancy) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['repeated injuries', 'late ANC visits', 'depression, anxiety', 'partner controlling behavior', 'substance use']
- pregnancy_substance_abuse (Substance Use in Pregnancy (Alcohol, Tobacco, Drugs)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['history of use', 'signs of intoxication or withdrawal', 'poor weight gain', 'fetal growth restriction', 'preterm labor']
- pregnancy_twin_twin_transfusion (Twin-Twin Transfusion Syndrome (TTTS)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['monochorionic twins with discordant size', 'polyhydramnios in one sac, oligohydramnios in the other', 'fetal distress', 'preterm labor', 'diagnosed by ultrasound']
- pregnancy_velamentous_umbilical (Velamentous Cord Insertion and Vasa Previa) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['cord inserts into membranes, not the placenta', 'vasa previa: fetal vessels cross over the cervical os', 'risk of fetal hemorrhage with membrane rupture']
- ovarian_torsion (Ovarian Torsion (Twisted Ovary)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden severe one-sided lower abdominal or pelvic pain', 'nausea and vomiting', 'pain may come in waves initially then become constant', 'low-grade fever', 'abdomen is tender on one side', 'pain may radiate to back or thigh']
- premature_ovarian_insufficiency (Premature Ovarian Insufficiency (POI) / Early Menopause) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['irregular or absent periods before age 40', 'hot flashes in a young woman', 'vaginal dryness', 'difficulty getting pregnant', 'mood changes', 'sleep disturbances', 'blood tests show high FSH and low estrogen']
- toxic_shock_syndrome (Toxic Shock Syndrome (TSS)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['sudden high fever (above 39°C)', 'low blood pressure causing dizziness or fainting', 'sunburn-like red rash especially on palms and soles', 'vomiting and diarrhea', 'muscle aches', 'redness of eyes, mouth, and throat', 'confusion', 'associated with prolonged tampon or menstrual cup use']
- pelvic_inflammatory_disease (Pelvic Inflammatory Disease (PID)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['lower abdominal or pelvic pain', 'fever', 'unusual vaginal discharge with bad odor', 'pain during sex', 'irregular bleeding', 'pain on urination', 'nausea and vomiting in severe cases', 'tenderness in lower abdomen']
- postmenopausal_bleeding (Postmenopausal Bleeding) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['any vaginal bleeding after 12 months of amenorrhea', 'spotting, heavy bleeding, or staining', 'may be pink, red, or brown']
- endometrial_hyperplasia (Endometrial Hyperplasia (Without Atypia)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['heavy menstrual bleeding', 'intermenstrual bleeding', 'postmenopausal bleeding', 'often asymptomatic']
- endometrial_cancer (Endometrial (Uterine) Cancer) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['postmenopausal bleeding (most common)', 'heavy abnormal bleeding in premenopausal women', 'pelvic pain', 'watery or blood-tinged discharge', 'pain during urination or sex']
- ovarian_cancer (Ovarian Cancer) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['early stages: often asymptomatic or vague', 'abdominal bloating or pressure', 'pelvic pain', 'difficulty eating or feeling full quickly', 'frequent urination', 'menstrual changes', 'weight loss']
- imperforate_hymen (Imperforate Hymen) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['no menstrual flow at puberty (primary amenorrhea)', 'cyclical abdominal pain (monthly)', 'bulging bluish hymen', 'urinary retention', 'pelvic mass (hematocolpos)']
- transverse_vaginal_septum (Transverse Vaginal Septum) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['primary amenorrhea', 'cyclical abdominal pain', 'may have normal external genitalia', 'difficulty with tampon use']
- catamenial_epilepsy (Catamenial Epilepsy (Seizures Related to Menstrual Cycle)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['increase in seizure frequency around menstruation (peri-menstrual or mid-cycle)', 'often in women with epilepsy', 'seizures are more frequent during the perimenstrual phase when estrogen drops']
- uterine_sarcoma (Uterine Sarcoma (Rare Uterine Malignancy)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['abnormal bleeding (postmenopausal or heavy)', 'rapidly growing uterus', 'pelvic pain', 'palpable mass', 'may have no symptoms']
- vaginal_cancer (Vaginal Cancer) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['vaginal bleeding (often postmenopausal)', 'watery or bloody discharge', 'pain or mass in vagina', 'pain during sex', 'urinary symptoms']
- vulvar_cancer (Vulvar Cancer) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['persistent vulvar itching', 'pain, burning, or bleeding', 'lump, sore, or ulcer on vulva', 'changes in skin color (white, red, or dark)', 'may be associated with lichen sclerosus or HPV']
- congenital_adrenal_hyperplasia (Congenital Adrenal Hyperplasia (CAH) – Menstrual Effects) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['ambiguous genitalia at birth (in classic)', 'primary amenorrhea or irregular periods', 'virilization (excess hair, deep voice)', 'infertility', 'salt-wasting crisis in infants']
- eating_disorder_amenorrhea (Eating Disorder-Induced Amenorrhea (Functional Hypothalamic Amenorrhea)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['amenorrhea for ≥3 months', 'weight loss, low BMI', 'excessive exercise', 'food restriction', 'stress', 'low estrogen (hot flashes, vaginal dryness)']
- catamenial_depression (Catamenial Depression (Depression Flaring with Menstrual Cycle)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['worsening of depressive symptoms in the premenstrual phase', 'increased irritability, sadness, anxiety', 'suicidal ideation may increase', 'not meeting full PMDD criteria but significant']
- iron_deficiency_anemia_menstrual (Iron Deficiency Anemia due to Heavy Menstrual Bleeding) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['fatigue, weakness, pale skin, dizziness, shortness of breath, palpitations, cold hands, brittle nails, pica (craving ice/dirt)']
- men_002 (Prostate Cancer (Adenocarcinoma)) -> Urgency: high

  Symptoms: ['craving for alcohol', 'loss of control over drinking', 'tolerance (needs more)', 'withdrawal symptoms (tremors, sweating, anxiety)', 'continued use despite problems']

- men_037 (Suicide Prevention in Men) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['talking about wanting to die', 'withdrawing from family and friends', 'increased substance use', 'mood swings, hopelessness', 'giving away possessions', 'making a plan']

  Symptoms: ['headache, dizziness, confusion', 'nausea, vomiting', 'visual disturbances', 'memory problems', 'loss of consciousness (may be brief)']

  Symptoms: ['heat exhaustion: heavy sweating, weakness, nausea, cramps', 'heat stroke: high temperature, confusion, hot dry skin, collapse']
- men_091 (Hyponatremia – Exertional (Men – Endurance Sports)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['nausea, vomiting, headache', 'confusion, seizures', 'muscle cramps, weakness', 'occurs in endurance athletes who drink excessive water without electrolytes']
- depression_major (Major Depressive Disorder) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['persistent sadness or emptiness for more than 2 weeks', 'loss of interest or pleasure in activities', 'significant weight loss/gain or appetite change', 'insomnia or hypersomnia', 'psychomotor agitation or retardation', 'fatigue or loss of energy', 'feelings of worthlessness or excessive guilt', 'diminished ability to think or concentrate', 'recurrent thoughts of death or suicide']
- bipolar_i (Bipolar I Disorder) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['manic episodes lasting at least 7 days (elevated mood, grandiosity, decreased need for sleep, racing thoughts, impulsivity, risky behavior)', 'depressive episodes (low mood, fatigue, suicidal thoughts)', 'sometimes psychotic features', 'significant impairment in functioning']
- bipolar_ii (Bipolar II Disorder) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['hypomanic episodes (less severe than mania, lasting at least 4 days)', 'major depressive episodes', 'no full manic episodes', 'may be misdiagnosed as depression']
- panic_disorder (Panic Disorder) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['recurrent unexpected panic attacks (sudden intense fear, palpitations, sweating, trembling, shortness of breath, chest pain, dizziness, fear of dying)', 'persistent concern about future attacks', 'avoidance of situations', 'agoraphobia may develop']
- agoraphobia (Agoraphobia) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['anxiety about places where escape might be difficult or help unavailable', 'typically fear of open spaces, crowds, public transport', 'avoidance leading to confinement to home', 'often with panic disorder']
- ptsd (Post-Traumatic Stress Disorder (PTSD)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['exposure to traumatic event (actual or threatened death, serious injury, sexual violence)', 'intrusive memories, nightmares, flashbacks', 'avoidance of reminders', 'negative alterations in mood and cognition', 'hyperarousal (hypervigilance, startle response, sleep disturbance)']
- acute_stress_disorder (Acute Stress Disorder) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['similar to PTSD but occurs within 1 month of trauma', 'dissociative symptoms (depersonalization, derealization)', 'intrusive symptoms', 'avoidance', 'hyperarousal', 'lasts 3 days to 1 month']
- schizophrenia (Schizophrenia) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['hallucinations (usually auditory)', 'delusions (fixed false beliefs)', 'disorganized speech and behavior', 'negative symptoms (flat affect, avolition, alogia, anhedonia, asociality)', 'cognitive impairment', 'onset usually in late adolescence to early adulthood']
- schizoaffective_disorder (Schizoaffective Disorder) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['features of schizophrenia (hallucinations, delusions, disorganized speech) and mood episode (mania or depression) occurring together', 'psychotic symptoms occur without mood episodes as well']
- brief_psychotic_disorder (Brief Psychotic Disorder) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['sudden onset of psychotic symptoms (delusions, hallucinations, disorganized speech) lasting 1 day to 1 month', 'full return to premorbid function', 'often triggered by stress']
- catatonia (Catatonia (Psychomotor Disturbance)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['stupor (no movement or speech)', 'mutism', 'posturing', 'negativism', 'excitement', 'echolalia/echopraxia', 'can occur in schizophrenia, mood disorders, or medical conditions']
- eating_disorder_anorexia (Anorexia Nervosa) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['restriction of energy intake leading to significantly low body weight', 'intense fear of gaining weight', 'disturbed body image', 'denial of seriousness of low weight', 'amenorrhea in females']
- eating_disorder_bulimia (Bulimia Nervosa) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['recurrent episodes of binge eating (large amount of food in short time with loss of control)', 'compensatory behaviors (vomiting, laxatives, fasting, excessive exercise)', 'self-evaluation unduly influenced by body shape/weight', 'occurs at least once a week for 3 months']
- substance_alcohol_use (Alcohol Use Disorder) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['strong craving for alcohol', 'loss of control over drinking', 'tolerance', 'withdrawal symptoms (tremors, sweating, seizures)', 'continued use despite problems', 'giving up activities']
- substance_opioid_use (Opioid Use Disorder) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['craving for opioids', 'impaired control over use', 'tolerance', 'withdrawal (pain, nausea, diarrhea, yawning, restlessness)', 'continued use despite harm']
- substance_stimulant_use (Stimulant Use Disorder (Cocaine, Amphetamines)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['strong craving, loss of control, tolerance, withdrawal (depression, fatigue, sleep changes)', 'social/occupational impairment', 'risky use']
- substance_inhalant_use (Inhalant Use Disorder) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['use of inhalants (glue, paint thinner, aerosols)', 'euphoria, dizziness, hallucinations', 'tolerance, withdrawal (sometimes)', 'neurological and cognitive damage']
- personality_disorder_borderline (Borderline Personality Disorder (BPD)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['unstable relationships', 'identity disturbance', 'impulsivity (e.g., self-harm, spending, substance)', 'affective instability', 'anger outbursts', 'chronic emptiness', 'paranoid ideation under stress', 'suicidal ideation']
- dissociative_identity_disorder (Dissociative Identity Disorder (DID)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['presence of two or more distinct identity states', 'recurrent gaps in memory for everyday events', 'depersonalization/derealization', 'often associated with severe trauma']
- paraphilia_disorder (Paraphilic Disorders (Pedophilic, Voyeuristic, Exhibitionistic, etc.)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['sexual arousal to atypical objects/situations, causing distress or impairment, or involving non-consenting persons', 'e.g., pedophilia, voyeurism, exhibitionism, frotteurism']
- pyromania (Pyromania) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['impulsive and deliberate fire-setting', 'tension before the act', 'fascination with fire', 'relief after setting fire', 'no other motive (e.g., financial gain, revenge)']
- body_dysmorphic_disorder (Body Dysmorphic Disorder (BDD)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['preoccupation with one or more perceived defects in appearance that are not observable or slight', 'repetitive behaviors (mirror checking, excessive grooming, seeking reassurance)', 'significant distress/impairment']
- delirium (Delirium (Acute Confusional State)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['acute onset of confusion, inattention, disorganized thinking, altered consciousness', 'fluctuating course', 'may have hallucinations, delusions, agitation, or lethargy', 'often due to medical condition, substance, or medication']
- dementia_alzheimer (Alzheimer's Dementia) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['gradual memory loss affecting daily life', 'difficulty with familiar tasks', 'confusion about time/place', 'language problems', 'poor judgment', 'personality changes', 'wandering', 'late: incontinence, swallowing difficulties']
- frontotemporal_dementia (Frontotemporal Dementia (FTD)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['personality and behavior changes (apathy, disinhibition, loss of empathy)', 'language difficulties (fluent aphasia)', 'early onset (40-60 years)', 'relatively preserved memory in early stages']
- lewy_body_dementia (Dementia with Lewy Bodies (DLB)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['dementia with prominent visual hallucinations', 'fluctuating cognition', 'Parkinsonian motor symptoms (rigidity, tremor)', 'REM sleep behavior disorder']
- parkinsons_disease_mental (Parkinson's Disease with Psychiatric Symptoms) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['motor symptoms (tremor, rigidity, bradykinesia)', 'depression, anxiety', 'psychosis (hallucinations, delusions)', 'cognitive decline', 'sleep disturbances']
- huntingtons_disease (Huntington's Disease) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['progressive chorea (involuntary movements)', 'psychiatric symptoms (depression, irritability, psychosis)', 'cognitive decline (dementia)', 'onset usually 30-50 years, autosomal dominant']
- substance_benzodiazepine_use (Benzodiazepine Use Disorder) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['craving, tolerance, withdrawal (anxiety, insomnia, seizures, tremor)', 'continued use despite problems', 'dose escalation']
- substance_sedative_use (Sedative, Hypnotic, or Anxiolytic Use Disorder) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['similar to benzodiazepine use', 'use of barbiturates or other sedatives', 'withdrawal can be life-threatening (delirium, seizures)']
- cutting_self_harm (Non-Suicidal Self-Injury (NSSI) – Cutting, Burning) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['deliberate self-inflicted damage to body tissue (cutting, burning, scratching) without suicidal intent', 'often to relieve tension or regulate emotions', 'may be associated with BPD or PTSD']
- factitious_disorder_imposed_on_another (Factitious Disorder Imposed on Another (Munchausen by Proxy)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['caregiver falsifies or induces illness in a person under their care (usually a child)', 'without external reward', 'perpetrator may have medical knowledge']
- mood_disorder_substance_induced (Substance-Induced Mood Disorder) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['depression or mania directly caused by substance intoxication or withdrawal', 'e.g., alcohol-induced depression, cocaine-induced mania']
- psychotic_disorder_substance_induced (Substance-Induced Psychotic Disorder) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['hallucinations or delusions due to substance use (e.g., cannabis, amphetamines)', 'onset during intoxication or withdrawal', 'resolves with abstinence']
- vitamin_a_deficiency (Vitamin A Deficiency (Xerophthalmia)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['night blindness (nyctalopia)', 'dry eyes (xerosis)', "Bitot's spots (foamy white patches on conjunctiva)", 'corneal ulceration', 'keratomalacia (softening of cornea)', 'dry skin (follicular hyperkeratosis)', 'increased susceptibility to infections']
- vitamin_d_rickets (Vitamin D Deficiency Rickets (Children)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['bowed legs (genu varum) or knock-knees (genu valgum)', 'widened wrists and ankles (rachitic rosary)', 'delayed walking and teething', 'muscle weakness, hypotonia', 'bone pain and tenderness', 'craniotabes (soft skull bones)', 'growth retardation', 'dental enamel defects']
- vitamin_k_deficiency (Vitamin K Deficiency) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['easy bruising', 'bleeding from gums or nose', 'prolonged bleeding from wounds', 'blood in urine or stool', 'heavy menstrual bleeding', 'in newborns: hemorrhagic disease of the newborn (bleeding from umbilical stump, GI bleed, intracranial hemorrhage)']
- vitamin_b1_beriberi (Thiamine (Vitamin B1) Deficiency – Beriberi) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['DRY BERIBERI: peripheral neuropathy (tingling, weakness, foot drop), muscle wasting, ataxia', 'WET BERIBERI: heart failure (dyspnea, edema, tachycardia), dilated cardiomyopathy', "WERNICKE'S ENCEPHALOPATHY: confusion, ophthalmoplegia (eye movement paralysis), ataxia, often in alcoholics", 'infantile beriberi: hoarse cry, vomiting, cyanosis, heart failure']
- vitamin_b3_pellagra (Niacin (Vitamin B3) Deficiency – Pellagra) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['classic 3 Ds: Dermatitis (rough, scaly rash on sun-exposed areas, hyperpigmentation with peeling), Diarrhea (glossitis, stomatitis, GI upset), Dementia (confusion, memory loss, depression, psychosis)', 'If untreated: death']
- vitamin_b12_deficiency (Vitamin B12 (Cobalamin) Deficiency) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['megaloblastic anemia (fatigue, pallor, shortness of breath)', 'peripheral neuropathy (numbness, tingling, especially hands and feet)', 'subacute combined degeneration of spinal cord (loss of vibration/position sense, ataxia, weakness)', 'glossitis (sore, red tongue)', 'cognitive impairment, memory loss', 'depression, confusion, psychosis', 'risk in strict vegetarians/vegans']
- iron_deficiency_anemia (Iron Deficiency Anemia) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['extreme fatigue, weakness', 'pale skin and nails', 'dizziness, shortness of breath', 'cold hands and feet', 'brittle, spoon-shaped nails (koilonychia)', 'headache', 'pica (craving ice, dirt, chalk)', 'restless legs syndrome', 'impaired cognitive function in children']
- iodine_deficiency (Iodine Deficiency Disorders (Goiter, Hypothyroidism)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['enlarged thyroid gland (goiter)', 'hypothyroidism: fatigue, weight gain, cold intolerance, constipation, dry skin', 'intellectual impairment (in children—cretinism)', 'growth retardation', 'hearing loss', 'pregnancy complications (miscarriage, stillbirth)']
- protein_energy_malnutrition_marasmus (Marasmus (Protein-Energy Malnutrition – Severe Wasting)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe weight loss, emaciation', 'muscle wasting (no subcutaneous fat)', 'skin and bones appearance (old man face)', 'hair thinning, easily pluckable', 'growth retardation', 'apathy, irritability', 'no edema (differentiates from kwashiorkor)']
- protein_energy_malnutrition_kwashiorkor (Kwashiorkor (Protein-Energy Malnutrition – Edematous)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['bilateral pitting edema (feet, hands, face)', 'skin changes: hyperpigmentation, desquamation, fissures (flaky paint dermatosis)', 'hair changes: reddish, brittle, sparse', 'potbelly (protuberant abdomen due to fatty liver)', 'muscle wasting (less than marasmus)', 'apathy, misery', 'fatty liver, hepatomegaly']
- marasmic_kwashiorkor (Marasmic-Kwashiorkor (Mixed Protein-Energy Malnutrition)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['severe wasting (marasmus) + edema (kwashiorkor)', 'very low weight-for-height', 'bilateral edema', 'skin and hair changes', 'hepatomegaly', 'apathy']
- severe_acute_malnutrition (Severe Acute Malnutrition (SAM) – General) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['MUAC < 11.5 cm (6-59 months)', 'weight-for-height Z-score < -3 SD', 'bilateral edema', 'severe wasting', 'frequent infections', 'poor appetite']
- moderate_acute_malnutrition (Moderate Acute Malnutrition (MAM)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['MUAC 11.5-12.5 cm (6-59 months)', 'weight-for-height Z-score between -2 and -3 SD', 'no edema', 'mild wasting', 'growth faltering']
- wasting_acute_malnutrition (Wasting (Acute Malnutrition)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['weight-for-height Z-score < -2 SD', 'rapid weight loss', 'visible ribs and collarbones', 'recent illness (diarrhea, infection)', 'MUAC decreased']
- metabolic_syndrome (Metabolic Syndrome (Nutrition-Related)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['central obesity (waist circumference ≥ 90 cm male, ≥ 80 cm female)', 'high triglycerides (≥150 mg/dL)', 'low HDL (<40 mg/dL male, <50 mg/dL female)', 'high BP (≥130/85 mmHg)', 'high fasting glucose (≥100 mg/dL)']
- food_allergy_general (Food Allergy (IgE-Mediated)) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['hives, itching', 'swelling of lips, tongue, face', 'wheezing, difficulty breathing', 'abdominal pain, vomiting, diarrhea', 'anaphylaxis (life-threatening)', 'symptoms occur within minutes to 2 hours of eating the offending food']
- cow_milk_protein_allergy (Cow's Milk Protein Allergy (CMPA) – Infant) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['vomiting, diarrhea (bloody in severe)', 'blood in stool', 'failure to thrive', 'eczema, hives', 'colicky crying', 'wheezing, respiratory symptoms', 'reflux']

  Symptoms: ['nausea, vomiting', 'headache', 'confusion', 'muscle cramps, weakness', 'seizures, coma (severe)', 'can occur with excessive water intake without electrolytes (e.g., marathon runners)']
- hyperkalemia_dietary (Hyperkalemia (High Potassium) – Dietary Excess or Renal Failure) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['muscle weakness, paralysis', 'numbness, tingling', 'palpitations, arrhythmias', 'nausea, vomiting', 'often asymptomatic until severe (cardiac arrest)']

  Symptoms: ['nausea, vomiting, constipation', 'fatigue, weakness', 'confusion, depression', 'polyuria, polydipsia', 'kidney stones, renal failure', 'can occur with excessive Vitamin D or calcium intake']

- sa_065 (Substance‑Induced Mood Disorder) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['depression, mania, or anxiety directly caused by substance intoxication or withdrawal', 'symptoms resolve with abstinence', 'e.g., alcohol‑induced depression, cocaine‑induced mania']
- sa_067 (Substance‑Induced Psychotic Disorder) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['hallucinations, delusions caused by substance use (stimulants, cannabis, hallucinogens)', 'onset during or soon after intoxication or withdrawal', 'resolves with abstinence (usually within 1 month)']
- sa_075 (Alcohol and Pregnancy – Fetal Alcohol Effects) -> Urgency: critical
  Symptoms: ['not a disease – prevention', 'alcohol during pregnancy can cause miscarriage, stillbirth, and FASD', 'no safe amount', 'stop drinking as soon as you know you are pregnant']
- sa_079 (Tobacco and Cancer Risk (Multiple Sites)) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['tobacco causes cancers of the lung, mouth, throat, esophagus, stomach, pancreas, kidney, bladder, and cervix']
- sa_084 (Alcohol and Pancreatitis – Prevention) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['recurrent abdominal pain, chronic pancreatitis']
- sa_086 (Withdrawal Management – General Principles) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['not a disease – management of detox', 'medical supervision, hydration, nutrition, and symptomatic medications']
- sa_092 (Dual Recovery – Mental Health and Substance Use) -> Urgency: high
  Symptoms: ['co‑occurring mental illness and substance use disorder']

  Symptoms: ['withdrawal from multiple substances simultaneously', 'complicated withdrawal syndrome', 'increased risk of seizures, delirium, and medical complications']
